THE TOURISM (LIST OF ACCOMODATION ESTABLISHMENTS) ORDER, 2021
Citation

Accommodation Establishments) Order, 2021
List of Hotels

GN No.
pay a bed night levy as prescribed in the Tourism Development
354 of 2013
Regulations.
Revocation of
3. The Tourism (List of Accommodation
GN No
Establishments) Order, 2020 is hereby revoked.
